# Test Case API Sang Loc Giao Dich (AML Transaction Screening)

**Endpoint:** `POST /transaction-screening/v1/transaction-screening`

**Nguon TC goc:** `requirements/MSB_AML_Testcase_API_TF_v1.1.xlsx` - sheet "API Sang Loc" (303 TC)  
**Nguon tai lieu:** `requirements/Thiet ke tich hop dien.xlsx`  
**Ngay sinh:** 2026-09-01

## Tong quan

| Chi so | So luong |
|---|---|
| TC giu nguyen tu file goc (khong sua cau chu) | 303 |
| TC bo sung (danh dau `[TC BO SUNG]` o cot Notes, to mau vang trong file .xlsx) | 24 |
| **Tong** | **327** |

> Cot `Notes` ghi ro nguon goc tung TC: `[GIU NGUYEN] ID goc: <ID> (dong <n>)` hoac
> `[TC BO SUNG] <tag> | Ly do: ...`. Toan bo TC cu duoc giu nguyen 100% cau chu o cac cot
> Test Case Summary / Pre-conditions / Test Data / Test Steps / Expected result - chi thay doi
> THU TU va duoc gan them Test Case ID moi theo chuan 4-phase.
